# Skills · D · part 27

785 entries, dataset-readiness-assessment to date-time-timezone-testing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dataset-readiness-assessment**: Profile a dataset, validate it against reviewed quality rules, and produce an owner-confirmed data dictionary. Use when a local CSV, TSV, JSON, or JSON Lines dataset must be assessed before analysis, ingestion, governance review, or handoff. ([Knuckles-Team/universal-skills](https://github.com/Knuckles-Team/universal-skills/tree/HEAD/universal_skills/skills/dataset-readiness-assessment) · ★4)
- **dataset-ready-check**: Validate that a sample is ready for release in this benchmark: it boots from a fresh clone at its pinned commit, an untouched tree reproduces the goldens, the cases are sensitive to real breakage, results are deterministic, the agent cannot see the answer key, at least one controller is fully covered, and the OpenTelemetry agent produces a usable trace base for `trace_similarity`. Use when asked to "check if this sample is dataset ready", "validate the sample", or "is this sample ready to publish". ([4xeverburga/jsp-decouple-bench](https://github.com/4xeverburga/jsp-decouple-bench/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/dataset-ready-check))
- **dataset-recipes**: Worked end-to-end examples of dataset ingests to copy from: PAD-US multi-layer GDB, Census TIGER per-state zips with a preprocess job, a single-COG carbon raster, reading one small table out of a huge remote zip via GDAL range reads, preprocessing multi-file zipped sources, and checking feature count and spatial coverage against the source before publishing. Use as a starting template when a new ingest resembles one of these shapes. ([boettiger-lab/data-workflows](https://github.com/boettiger-lab/data-workflows/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dataset-recipes) · ★5)
- **dataset-recon**: Reconnaissance on an unfamiliar dataset from a URL or local file. Gathers its docs, metadata and specs, even when they are scattered or decades old, pulls a small sample, decodes it with the proper reader (GDAL, wgrib2, ncdump, h5dump, pandas, pyarrow), and writes a short profile covering what the data is, the variables and parameters, grid size or resolution, file sizes, update frequency, and undocumented attributes or quirks. Use when the user points at a raw CSV with no docs, a GRIB2/NetCDF/HDF5 weather or climate product (NDFD, GFS, HRRR, ERA5), a data portal or directory listing, and ask… ([kremit/skills](https://github.com/kremit/skills/tree/HEAD/data/dataset-recon))
- **dataset-reconciler**: Compare two datasets by key, isolate missing rows and field-level differences, and summarize reconciliation exceptions clearly. ([nicholashidalgo/claude-skillforge](https://github.com/nicholashidalgo/claude-skillforge/tree/HEAD/data-engineering/dataset-reconciler) · ★1)
- **dataset-refresh**: Configure, trigger, monitor, and troubleshoot Power BI dataset refresh operations. ([Insightpulseai/powerbi-skills](https://github.com/Insightpulseai/powerbi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dataset-refresh))
- **dataset-register**: Use when: after /data-acquisition, formalize staged dataset in topic catalog (provenance, variables, quirks). NOT downloading (/data-acquisition). ([markusstrasser/skills](https://github.com/markusstrasser/skills/tree/HEAD/dataset-register))

Даёт actionable-фидбэк (что норм / что не продумано / корректировки) + мягкое резюме, без жёсткого pass/fail. Domain-agnostic: фундаментальные паттерны обязательны, конкретные типы — гибко под домен. - **dataviz-precision**: Decide how many significant digits to show for numbers in a chart or a table - axis ticks, data labels, and table cells. Use whenever numeric values are displayed and you must choose precision: how many digits on an axis, how many decimals on a bar label, how to round a column so it reads cleanly. Precision is keyed to the spread of the data (max minus min), not to individual values, and is expressed as significant digits, not decimal places; every value in a column or axis is rounded to one uniform place.
